    SELECT [p0].[PedidoDetalleID], [p0].[Cantidad], [p0].[ColorID], [p0].[PedidoID], [p0].[PrecioUnitario], [p0].[ProductoID], [p0].[Subtotal], [p0].[TallaID], [p1].[ProductoID] AS [ProductoID0], [p1].[CategoriaPrincipalID], [p1].[Codigo], [p1].[Descripcion], [p1].[DescripcionCorta], [p1].[Estado], [p1].[FechaActualizacion], [p1].[FechaCreacion], [p1].[ImagenPrincipal], [p1].[MarcaID], [p1].[Nombre], [p1].[PrecioCompra], [p1].[PrecioOferta], [p1].[PrecioVenta], [p1].[Stock], [p1].[TipoProductoID], [t].[TallaID] AS [TallaID0], [t].[Nombre] AS [Nombre0], [t].[Orden], [c].[ColorID] AS [ColorID0], [c].[CodigoHex], [c].[Estado] AS [Estado0], [c].[Nombre] AS [Nombre1]
    FROM [PedidoDetalles] AS [p0]
    INNER JOIN [Productos] AS [p1] ON [p0].[ProductoID] = [p1].[ProductoID]
    LEFT JOIN [Tallas] AS [t] ON [p0].[TallaID] = [t].[TallaID]
    LEFT JOIN [Colores] AS [c] ON [p0].[ColorID] = [c].[ColorID]
) AS [t0] ON [p].[PedidoID] = [t0].[PedidoID]
ORDER BY [p].[FechaPedido] DESC, [p].[PedidoID], [t0].[PedidoDetalleID], [t0].[ProductoID0], [t0].[TallaID0]
